// js/systems/fishing.js //================================================ // HERO OF WIND LAND // FISHING SYSTEM V20 //================================================ const FishingSystemV20 = { enabled: true, fishingSpots: new Map(), fishDatabase: new Map(), activeSessions: new Map(), statistics: { casts: 0, catches: 0, failed: 0, rareCatches: 0, perfectCatches: 0, totalValue: 0 }, config: { baseBiteDelayMin: 1200, baseBiteDelayMax: 4500, catchWindow: 2200, perfectWindow: 500, defaultRespawn: 60000, rareBonusPerLuck: 0.0025 } }; //================================================ // FISH RARITY //================================================ const FishRarityV20 = { COMMON: "common", UNCOMMON: "uncommon", RARE: "rare", EPIC: "epic", LEGENDARY: "legendary", MYTHIC: "mythic" }; //================================================ // FISH TYPE //================================================ const FishTypeV20 = { FRESHWATER: "freshwater", SALTWATER: "saltwater", RIVER: "river", LAKE: "lake", OCEAN: "ocean", MAGICAL: "magical" }; //================================================ // FISH CLASS //================================================ class FishItemV20 { constructor(config = {}) { this.id = config.id || "fish_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2); this.name = config.name || "Unknown Fish"; this.type = config.type || FishTypeV20.FRESHWATER; this.rarity = config.rarity || FishRarityV20.COMMON; this.requiredLevel = Math.max( 1, Number( config.requiredLevel ) || 1 ); this.minAmount = Math.max( 1, Number( config.minAmount ) || 1 ); this.maxAmount = Math.max( this.minAmount, Number( config.maxAmount ) || this.minAmount ); this.value = Math.max( 0, Number( config.value ) || 5 ); this.exp = Math.max( 0, Number( config.exp ) || 5 ); this.weightMin = Math.max( 0.01, Number( config.weightMin ) || 0.1 ); this.weightMax = Math.max( this.weightMin, Number( config.weightMax ) || this.weightMin ); this.description = config.description || ""; } } //================================================ // FISHING SPOT CLASS //================================================ class FishingSpotV20 { constructor( id, config = {} ) { this.id = id; this.name = config.name || "Fishing Spot"; this.x = Number( config.x ) || 0; this.y = Number( config.y ) || 0; this.radius = Math.max( 20, Number( config.radius ) || 100 ); this.type = config.type || FishTypeV20.FRESHWATER; this.requiredLevel = Math.max( 1, Number( config.requiredLevel ) || 1 ); this.fishTable = Array.isArray( config.fishTable ) ? [ ...config.fishTable ] : []; this.maxFish = Math.max( 1, Number( config.maxFish ) || 20 ); this.fishLeft = Number.isFinite( Number( config.fishLeft ) ) ? Math.max( 0, Number( config.fishLeft ) ) : this.maxFish; this.depleted = this.fishLeft <= 0; this.respawnAt = Number( config.respawnAt ) || 0; this.respawnTime = Math.max( 1000, Number( config.respawnTime ) || FishingSystemV20 .config .defaultRespawn ); } } //================================================ // REGISTER FISH //================================================ function registerFishV20( config ) { const fish = config instanceof FishItemV20 ? config : new FishItemV20( config ); FishingSystemV20 .fishDatabase .set( fish.id, fish ); return fish; } //================================================ // GET FISH //================================================ function getFishV20( fishId ) { return ( FishingSystemV20 .fishDatabase .get( fishId ) || null ); } //================================================ // REGISTER SPOT //================================================ function registerFishingSpotV20( config ) { if ( !config || !config.id ) { return null; } const spot = config instanceof FishingSpotV20 ? config : new FishingSpotV20( config.id, config ); FishingSystemV20 .fishingSpots .set( spot.id, spot ); return spot; } //================================================ // GET SPOT //================================================ function getFishingSpotV20( spotId ) { return ( FishingSystemV20 .fishingSpots .get( spotId ) || null ); } //================================================ // PLAYER FISHING LEVEL //================================================ function getFishingLevelV20() { const player = typeof getPlayerSkillCaster === "function" ? getPlayerSkillCaster() : null; if (!player) return 1; return Math.max( 1, Number( player.fishingLevel ) || Number( player.gatheringLevel ) || Number( player.level ) || 1 ); } //================================================ // PLAYER FISHING LUCK //================================================ function getFishingLuckV20() { const player = typeof getPlayerSkillCaster === "function" ? getPlayerSkillCaster() : null; if (!player) return 0; return Math.max( 0, Number( player.fishingLuck ) || Number( player.luck ) || 0 ); } //================================================ // PLAYER FISHING SPEED //================================================ function getFishingSpeedV20() { const player = typeof getPlayerSkillCaster === "function" ? getPlayerSkillCaster() : null; if (!player) return 0; return Math.max( 0, Number( player.fishingSpeed ) || 0 ); } //================================================ // CHECK FISHING ROD //================================================ function hasFishingRodV20() { if ( typeof getInventoryItemCountV9 !== "function" ) { return true; } const rods = [ "fishing_rod", "wooden_fishing_rod", "iron_fishing_rod", "golden_fishing_rod", "mythic_fishing_rod" ]; for ( const rod of rods ) { if ( getInventoryItemCountV9( rod ) > 0 ) { return true; } } return false; } //================================================ // UPDATE SPOT //================================================ function updateFishingSpotV20( spot ) { if (!spot) return null; if ( !spot.depleted ) { return spot; } if ( Date.now() >= spot.respawnAt ) { spot.fishLeft = spot.maxFish; spot.depleted = false; spot.respawnAt = 0; } return spot; } //================================================ // SELECT FISH //================================================ function selectFishV20( spot ) { if (!spot) return null; const level = getFishingLevelV20(); const luck = getFishingLuckV20(); const candidates = []; for ( const entry of spot.fishTable ) { const fish = getFishV20( entry.id ); if (!fish) continue; if ( level < fish.requiredLevel ) { continue; } let weight = Math.max( 0, Number( entry.weight ) || 1 ); if ( fish.rarity !== FishRarityV20.COMMON ) { weight *= 1 + luck * FishingSystemV20 .config .rareBonusPerLuck; } candidates.push({ fish, weight }); } if ( !candidates.length ) { return null; } const totalWeight = candidates.reduce( ( sum, entry ) => sum + entry.weight, 0 ); let roll = Math.random() * totalWeight; for ( const entry of candidates ) { roll -= entry.weight; if ( roll <= 0 ) { return entry.fish; } } return candidates[ candidates.length - 1 ].fish; } //================================================ // RANDOM BITE DELAY //================================================ function getFishingBiteDelayV20() { const speed = getFishingSpeedV20(); const min = FishingSystemV20 .config .baseBiteDelayMin; const max = FishingSystemV20 .config .baseBiteDelayMax; const base = min + Math.random() * ( max - min ); return Math.max( 400, base * ( 1 - Math.min( 0.6, speed * 0.01 ) ) ); } //================================================ // CAST LINE //================================================ function startFishingV20( spotId ) { const spot = getFishingSpotV20( spotId ); if (!spot) { FishingSystemV20 .statistics .failed++; return { success: false, reason: "spot_not_found" }; } updateFishingSpotV20( spot ); if ( spot.depleted ) { return { success: false, reason: "spot_depleted", respawnAt: spot.respawnAt }; } if ( getFishingLevelV20() < spot.requiredLevel ) { return { success: false, reason: "level_too_low" }; } if ( !hasFishingRodV20() ) { return { success: false, reason: "rod_missing" }; } if ( FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .has( spotId ) ) { return { success: false, reason: "already_fishing" }; } const fish = selectFishV20( spot ); if (!fish) { return { success: false, reason: "no_available_fish" }; } const now = Date.now(); const biteDelay = getFishingBiteDelayV20(); const session = { spotId, fishId: fish.id, state: "waiting", startedAt: now, biteAt: now + biteDelay, catchDeadline: 0, biteDelay, caught: false, perfect: false }; FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .set( spotId, session ); FishingSystemV20 .statistics .casts++; return { success: true, spotId, state: session.state, biteAt: session.biteAt }; } //================================================ // UPDATE FISHING SESSION //================================================ function updateFishingV20( spotId ) { const session = FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .get( spotId ); if (!session) return null; const now = Date.now(); if ( session.state === "waiting" ) { if ( now >= session.biteAt ) { session.state = "biting"; session.biteStartedAt = now; session.catchDeadline = now + FishingSystemV20 .config .catchWindow; } } if ( session.state === "biting" ) { if ( now > session.catchDeadline ) { session.state = "failed"; FishingSystemV20 .statistics .failed++; } } return { ...session, remaining: session.state === "waiting" ? Math.max( 0, session.biteAt - now ) : session.state === "biting" ? Math.max( 0, session.catchDeadline - now ) : 0 }; } //================================================ // HOOK FISH //================================================ function hookFishV20( spotId ) { const session = FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .get( spotId ); if (!session) { return { success: false, reason: "no_active_session" }; } updateFishingV20( spotId ); if ( session.state !== "biting" ) { return { success: false, reason: session.state === "waiting" ? "fish_not_biting" : "fish_escaped" }; } const reactionTime = Date.now() - session.biteStartedAt; session.perfect = reactionTime <= FishingSystemV20 .config .perfectWindow; session.state = "hooked"; session.caught = true; return { success: true, perfect: session.perfect, reactionTime }; } //================================================ // COMPLETE CATCH //================================================ function completeFishingV20( spotId ) { const session = FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .get( spotId ); if (!session) { return { success: false, reason: "no_active_session" }; } updateFishingV20( spotId ); if ( session.state === "failed" ) { FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .delete( spotId ); return { success: false, reason: "fish_escaped" }; } if ( !session.caught || session.state !== "hooked" ) { return { success: false, reason: "fish_not_hooked" }; } const fish = getFishV20( session.fishId ); const spot = getFishingSpotV20( spotId ); FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .delete( spotId ); if ( !fish || !spot ) { FishingSystemV20 .statistics .failed++; return { success: false, reason: "catch_data_missing" }; } let amount = fish.minAmount + Math.floor( Math.random() * ( fish.maxAmount - fish.minAmount + 1 ) ); if ( session.perfect ) { amount++; FishingSystemV20 .statistics .perfectCatches++; } const weight = fish.weightMin + Math.random() * ( fish.weightMax - fish.weightMin ); const roundedWeight = Math.round( weight * 100 ) / 100; if ( typeof addInventoryItemV9 === "function" ) { addInventoryItemV9( fish.id, amount ); } const player = typeof getPlayerSkillCaster === "function" ? getPlayerSkillCaster() : null; const gainedExp = session.perfect ? Math.floor( fish.exp * 1.5 ) : fish.exp; if (player) { if ( typeof player.exp === "number" ) { player.exp += gainedExp; } if ( typeof player.experience === "number" ) { player.experience += gainedExp; } } spot.fishLeft = Math.max( 0, spot.fishLeft - 1 ); if ( spot.fishLeft <= 0 ) { spot.depleted = true; spot.respawnAt = Date.now() + spot.respawnTime; } FishingSystemV20 .statistics .catches++; FishingSystemV20 .statistics .totalValue += fish.value * amount; if ( fish.rarity !== FishRarityV20.COMMON ) { FishingSystemV20 .statistics .rareCatches++; } return { success: true, fishId: fish.id, fishName: fish.name, rarity: fish.rarity, amount, weight: roundedWeight, perfect: session.perfect, exp: gainedExp, value: fish.value * amount, fishLeft: spot.fishLeft, depleted: spot.depleted, respawnAt: spot.respawnAt }; } //================================================ // CANCEL FISHING //================================================ function cancelFishingV20( spotId ) { if ( !FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .has( spotId ) ) { return false; } FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .delete( spotId ); return true; } //================================================ // FIND NEAREST SPOT //================================================ function findNearestFishingSpotV20( x, y, maxDistance = 180 ) { let nearest = null; let nearestDistance = Infinity; for ( const spot of FishingSystemV20 .fishingSpots .values() ) { updateFishingSpotV20( spot ); if ( spot.depleted ) continue; const dx = spot.x - x; const dy = spot.y - y; const distance = Math.sqrt( dx * dx + dy * dy ); if ( distance <= maxDistance && distance < nearestDistance ) { nearest = spot; nearestDistance = distance; } } return nearest; } //================================================ // DEFAULT FISH //================================================ registerFishV20({ id: "small_carp", name: "Small Carp", type: FishTypeV20.RIVER, rarity: FishRarityV20.COMMON, minAmount: 1, maxAmount: 2, value: 12, exp: 8, weightMin: 0.2, weightMax: 1.2 }); registerFishV20({ id: "river_trout", name: "River Trout", type: FishTypeV20.RIVER, rarity: FishRarityV20.UNCOMMON, requiredLevel: 8, value: 45, exp: 18, weightMin: 0.8, weightMax: 3.5 }); registerFishV20({ id: "golden_koi", name: "Golden Koi", type: FishTypeV20.LAKE, rarity: FishRarityV20.RARE, requiredLevel: 20, value: 220, exp: 60, weightMin: 1, weightMax: 8 }); registerFishV20({ id: "crystal_fish", name: "Crystal Fish", type: FishTypeV20.MAGICAL, rarity: FishRarityV20.EPIC, requiredLevel: 35, value: 800, exp: 160, weightMin: 0.5, weightMax: 4 }); registerFishV20({ id: "storm_serpent", name: "Storm Serpent", type: FishTypeV20.MAGICAL, rarity: FishRarityV20.LEGENDARY, requiredLevel: 50, value: 5000, exp: 600, weightMin: 5, weightMax: 30 }); registerFishV20({ id: "celestial_leviathan", name: "Celestial Leviathan", type: FishTypeV20.MAGICAL, rarity: FishRarityV20.MYTHIC, requiredLevel: 70, value: 50000, exp: 3000, weightMin: 50, weightMax: 250 }); //================================================ // DEFAULT FISHING SPOTS //================================================ registerFishingSpotV20({ id: "river_spot_01", name: "Whispering River", x: 650, y: 900, radius: 160, type: FishTypeV20.RIVER, maxFish: 30, fishTable: [ { id: "small_carp", weight: 75 }, { id: "river_trout", weight: 25 } ] }); registerFishingSpotV20({ id: "lake_spot_01", name: "Moon Lake", x: 2200, y: 1500, radius: 220, type: FishTypeV20.LAKE, requiredLevel: 10, maxFish: 25, respawnTime: 120000, fishTable: [ { id: "small_carp", weight: 30 }, { id: "river_trout", weight: 45 }, { id: "golden_koi", weight: 25 } ] }); registerFishingSpotV20({ id: "crystal_lake_01", name: "Crystal Lake", x: 3600, y: 2600, radius: 250, type: FishTypeV20.MAGICAL, requiredLevel: 35, maxFish: 20, respawnTime: 180000, fishTable: [ { id: "golden_koi", weight: 40 }, { id: "crystal_fish", weight: 55 }, { id: "storm_serpent", weight: 5 } ] }); registerFishingSpotV20({ id: "celestial_sea_01", name: "Celestial Sea", x: 6200, y: 4200, radius: 400, type: FishTypeV20.MAGICAL, requiredLevel: 65, maxFish: 12, respawnTime: 600000, fishTable: [ { id: "crystal_fish", weight: 40 }, { id: "storm_serpent", weight: 45 }, { id: "celestial_leviathan", weight: 15 } ] }); //================================================ // UPDATE ALL SPOTS //================================================ function updateAllFishingSpotsV20() { for ( const spot of FishingSystemV20 .fishingSpots .values() ) { updateFishingSpotV20( spot ); } } //================================================ // SAVE DATA //================================================ function getFishingSaveDataV20() { return { statistics: { ...FishingSystemV20 .statistics }, spots: Array.from( FishingSystemV20 .fishingSpots .values() ).map( spot => ({ id: spot.id, fishLeft: spot.fishLeft, maxFish: spot.maxFish, depleted: spot.depleted, respawnAt: spot.respawnAt }) ) }; } //================================================ // LOAD DATA //================================================ function loadFishingSaveDataV20( data ) { if (!data) return; if ( data.statistics ) { FishingSystemV20 .statistics = { ...FishingSystemV20 .statistics, ...data.statistics }; } if ( Array.isArray( data.spots ) ) { for ( const saved of data.spots ) { const spot = getFishingSpotV20( saved.id ); if (!spot) continue; spot.fishLeft = Math.max( 0, Number( saved.fishLeft ) ); spot.maxFish = Number( saved.maxFish ) || spot.maxFish; spot.depleted = Boolean( saved.depleted ); spot.respawnAt = Number( saved.respawnAt ) || 0; } } updateAllFishingSpotsV20(); } //================================================ // EXPORT //================================================ if ( typeof window !== "undefined" ) { window.FishingSystemV20 = FishingSystemV20; window.FishRarityV20 = FishRarityV20; window.FishTypeV20 = FishTypeV20; window.FishItemV20 = FishItemV20; window.FishingSpotV20 = FishingSpotV20; window.registerFishV20 = registerFishV20; window.getFishV20 = getFishV20; window.registerFishingSpotV20 = registerFishingSpotV20; window.getFishingSpotV20 = getFishingSpotV20; window.getFishingLevelV20 = getFishingLevelV20; window.getFishingLuckV20 = getFishingLuckV20; window.startFishingV20 = startFishingV20; window.updateFishingV20 = updateFishingV20; window.hookFishV20 = hookFishV20; window.completeFishingV20 = completeFishingV20; window.cancelFishingV20 = cancelFishingV20; window.findNearestFishingSpotV20 = findNearestFishingSpotV20; window.updateFishingSpotV20 = updateFishingSpotV20; window.updateAllFishingSpotsV20 = updateAllFishingSpotsV20; window.getFishingSaveDataV20 = getFishingSaveDataV20; window.loadFishingSaveDataV20 = loadFishingSaveDataV20; } //================================================ // AUTO UPDATE //================================================ if ( typeof setInterval === "function" ) { setInterval( () => { if ( !FishingSystemV20 .enabled ) { return; } updateAllFishingSpotsV20(); for ( const spotId of FishingSystemV20 .activeSessions .keys() ) { updateFishingV20( spotId ); } }, 150 ); } //================================================ // END FISHING SYSTEM V20 //================================================ // js/systems/mining.js //================================================ // HERO OF WIND LAND // MINING SYSTEM V21 //================================================ const MiningSystemV21 = { enabled: true, nodes: new Map(), ores: new Map(), activeSessions: new Map(), statistics: { swings: 0, successfulMines: 0, failedMines: 0, criticalMines: 0, rareOres: 0, totalOres: 0, totalValue: 0 }, config: { baseMineTime: 1800, minMineTime: 350, maxMineTime: 6000, criticalChance: 0.08, criticalMultiplier: 2, defaultRespawn: 120000 } }; //================================================ // ORE RARITY //================================================ const OreRarityV21 = { COMMON: "common", UNCOMMON: "uncommon", RARE: "rare", EPIC: "epic", LEGENDARY: "legendary", MYTHIC: "mythic" }; //================================================ // ORE CLASS //================================================ class OreItemV21 { constructor(config = {}) { this.id = config.id || "ore_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2); this.name = config.name || "Unknown Ore"; this.rarity = config.rarity || OreRarityV21.COMMON; this.requiredLevel = Math.max( 1, Number(config.requiredLevel) || 1 ); this.minAmount = Math.max( 1, Number(config.minAmount) || 1 ); this.maxAmount = Math.max( this.minAmount, Number(config.maxAmount) || this.minAmount ); this.value = Math.max( 0, Number(config.value) || 1 ); this.exp = Math.max( 0, Number(config.exp) || 1 ); this.description = config.description || ""; } } //================================================ // MINING NODE CLASS //================================================ class MiningNodeV21 { constructor(id, config = {}) { this.id = id; this.name = config.name || "Ore Vein"; this.x = Number(config.x) || 0; this.y = Number(config.y) || 0; this.radius = Math.max( 20, Number(config.radius) || 40 ); this.requiredLevel = Math.max( 1, Number(config.requiredLevel) || 1 ); this.oreTable = Array.isArray(config.oreTable) ? [...config.oreTable] : []; this.maxDurability = Math.max( 1, Number(config.maxDurability) || 5 ); this.durability = Number.isFinite( Number(config.durability) ) ? Math.max(0, Number(config.durability)) : this.maxDurability; this.depleted = this.durability <= 0; this.respawnTime = Math.max( 1000, Number(config.respawnTime) || MiningSystemV21.config.defaultRespawn ); this.respawnAt = Number(config.respawnAt) || 0; } } //================================================ // REGISTER ORE //================================================ function registerOreV21(config) { const ore = config instanceof OreItemV21 ? config : new OreItemV21(config); MiningSystemV21.ores.set( ore.id, ore ); return ore; } //================================================ // GET ORE //================================================ function getOreV21(id) { return MiningSystemV21.ores.get(id) || null; } //================================================ // REGISTER MINING NODE //================================================ function registerMiningNodeV21(config) { if (!config || !config.id) { return null; } const node = config instanceof MiningNodeV21 ? config : new MiningNodeV21( config.id, config ); MiningSystemV21.nodes.set( node.id, node ); return node; } //================================================ // GET MINING NODE //================================================ function getMiningNodeV21(id) { return MiningSystemV21.nodes.get(id) || null; } //================================================ // GET PLAYER //================================================ function getMiningPlayerV21() { return typeof getPlayerSkillCaster === "function" ? getPlayerSkillCaster() : null; } //================================================ // MINING LEVEL //================================================ function getMiningLevelV21() { const player = getMiningPlayerV21(); if (!player) { return 1; } return Math.max( 1, Number(player.miningLevel) || Number(player.gatheringLevel) || Number(player.level) || 1 ); } //================================================ // MINING POWER //================================================ function getMiningPowerV21() { const player = getMiningPlayerV21(); if (!player) { return 1; } return Math.max( 1, Number(player.miningPower) || Number(player.strength) || Number(player.attack) || 1 ); } //================================================ // MINING LUCK //================================================ function getMiningLuckV21() { const player = getMiningPlayerV21(); if (!player) { return 0; } return Math.max( 0, Number(player.miningLuck) || Number(player.luck) || 0 ); } //================================================ // PICKAXE CHECK //================================================ function hasPickaxeV21() { if ( typeof getInventoryItemCountV9 !== "function" ) { return true; } const pickaxes = [ "wooden_pickaxe", "stone_pickaxe", "iron_pickaxe", "steel_pickaxe", "golden_pickaxe", "crystal_pickaxe", "mythic_pickaxe", "pickaxe" ]; for (const pickaxe of pickaxes) { if ( getInventoryItemCountV9(pickaxe) > 0 ) { return true; } } return false; } //================================================ // UPDATE NODE //================================================ function updateMiningNodeV21(node) { if (!node) { return null; } if (!node.depleted) { return node; } if (Date.now() >= node.respawnAt) { node.durability = node.maxDurability; node.depleted = false; node.respawnAt = 0; } return node; } //================================================ // SELECT ORE //================================================ function selectOreV21(node) { if (!node) { return null; } const level = getMiningLevelV21(); const luck = getMiningLuckV21(); const candidates = []; for (const entry of node.oreTable) { const ore = getOreV21(entry.id); if (!ore) { continue; } if (level < ore.requiredLevel) { continue; } let weight = Math.max( 0, Number(entry.weight) || 1 ); if ( ore.rarity !== OreRarityV21.COMMON ) { weight *= 1 + luck * 0.002; } candidates.push({ ore, weight }); } if (!candidates.length) { return null; } const totalWeight = candidates.reduce( (sum, entry) => sum + entry.weight, 0 ); let roll = Math.random() * totalWeight; for (const entry of candidates) { roll -= entry.weight; if (roll <= 0) { return entry.ore; } } return candidates[ candidates.length - 1 ].ore; } //================================================ // CALCULATE MINING TIME //================================================ function getMiningTimeV21() { const power = getMiningPowerV21(); const time = MiningSystemV21.config.baseMineTime / ( 1 + power * 0.05 ); return Math.max( MiningSystemV21.config.minMineTime, Math.min( MiningSystemV21.config.maxMineTime, time ) ); } //================================================ // START MINING //================================================ function startMiningV21(nodeId) { const node = getMiningNodeV21(nodeId); if (!node) { MiningSystemV21.statistics.failedMines++; return { success: false, reason: "node_not_found" }; } updateMiningNodeV21(node); if (node.depleted) { return { success: false, reason: "node_depleted", respawnAt: node.respawnAt }; } if ( getMiningLevelV21() < node.requiredLevel ) { return { success: false, reason: "level_too_low" }; } if (!hasPickaxeV21()) { return { success: false, reason: "pickaxe_missing" }; } if ( MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.has(nodeId) ) { return { success: false, reason: "already_mining" }; } const ore = selectOreV21(node); if (!ore) { return { success: false, reason: "no_available_ore" }; } const now = Date.now(); const duration = getMiningTimeV21(); const session = { nodeId, oreId: ore.id, startedAt: now, finishAt: now + duration, duration, completed: false }; MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.set( nodeId, session ); MiningSystemV21.statistics.swings++; return { success: true, nodeId, oreId: ore.id, finishAt: session.finishAt, duration }; } //================================================ // UPDATE MINING SESSION //================================================ function updateMiningV21(nodeId) { const session = MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.get(nodeId); if (!session) { return null; } const remaining = Math.max( 0, session.finishAt - Date.now() ); if (remaining <= 0) { session.completed = true; } return { ...session, remaining, ready: remaining <= 0 }; } //================================================ // COMPLETE MINING //================================================ function completeMiningV21(nodeId) { const session = MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.get(nodeId); if (!session) { return { success: false, reason: "no_active_session" }; } if (Date.now() < session.finishAt) { return { success: false, reason: "mining_not_finished", remaining: session.finishAt - Date.now() }; } const node = getMiningNodeV21(nodeId); const ore = getOreV21(session.oreId); MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.delete(nodeId); if (!node || !ore) { MiningSystemV21.statistics.failedMines++; return { success: false, reason: "mining_data_missing" }; } const luck = getMiningLuckV21(); const criticalChance = Math.min( 0.5, MiningSystemV21.config.criticalChance + luck * 0.003 ); const critical = Math.random() < criticalChance; let amount = ore.minAmount + Math.floor( Math.random() * ( ore.maxAmount - ore.minAmount + 1 ) ); if (critical) { amount *= MiningSystemV21.config.criticalMultiplier; MiningSystemV21.statistics.criticalMines++; } if ( typeof addInventoryItemV9 === "function" ) { addInventoryItemV9( ore.id, amount ); } const player = getMiningPlayerV21(); const gainedExp = critical ? Math.floor(ore.exp * 1.5) : ore.exp; if (player) { if ( typeof player.exp === "number" ) { player.exp += gainedExp; } if ( typeof player.experience === "number" ) { player.experience += gainedExp; } } node.durability = Math.max( 0, node.durability - 1 ); if (node.durability <= 0) { node.depleted = true; node.respawnAt = Date.now() + node.respawnTime; } MiningSystemV21.statistics.successfulMines++; MiningSystemV21.statistics.totalOres += amount; MiningSystemV21.statistics.totalValue += ore.value * amount; if ( ore.rarity !== OreRarityV21.COMMON ) { MiningSystemV21.statistics.rareOres++; } return { success: true, oreId: ore.id, oreName: ore.name, rarity: ore.rarity, amount, critical, exp: gainedExp, value: ore.value * amount, durability: node.durability, maxDurability: node.maxDurability, depleted: node.depleted, respawnAt: node.respawnAt }; } //================================================ // CANCEL MINING //================================================ function cancelMiningV21(nodeId) { if ( !MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.has(nodeId) ) { return false; } MiningSystemV21.activeSessions.delete(nodeId); return true; } //================================================ // FIND NEAREST NODE //================================================ function findNearestMiningNodeV21( x, y, maxDistance = 150 ) { let nearest = null; let nearestDistance = Infinity; for ( const node of MiningSystemV21.nodes.values() ) { updateMiningNodeV21(node); if (node.depleted) { continue; } const dx = node.x - x; const dy = node.y - y; const distance = Math.sqrt( dx * dx + dy * dy ); if (!player) { return 0; } return Math.max( 0, Number(player.herbalismLuck) || Number(player.gatheringLuck) || Number(player.luck) || 0 ); } //================================================ // UPDATE HERB PATCH //================================================ function updateHerbPatchV23(patch) { if (!patch) { return null; } if (!patch.depleted) { return patch; } if ( Date.now() >= patch.respawnAt ) { patch.harvestsLeft = patch.maxHarvests; patch.depleted = false; patch.respawnAt = 0; } return patch; } //================================================ // SELECT HERB //================================================ function selectHerbV23(patch) { if (!patch) { return null; } const level = getHerbalismLevelV23(); const luck = getHerbalismLuckV23(); const candidates = []; for ( const entry of patch.herbTable ) { const herb = getHerbV23(entry.id); if (!herb) { continue; } if ( level < herb.requiredLevel ) { continue; } let weight = Math.max( 0, Number(entry.weight) || 1 ); if ( herb.rarity !== HerbRarityV23.COMMON ) { weight *= 1 + luck * HerbalismSystemV23 .config .rareLuckBonus; } candidates.push({ herb, weight }); } if (!candidates.length) { return null; } const totalWeight = candidates.reduce( (sum, entry) => sum + entry.weight, 0 ); let roll = Math.random() * totalWeight; for ( const entry of candidates ) { roll -= entry.weight; if (roll <= 0) { return entry.herb; } } return candidates[ candidates.length - 1 ].herb; } //================================================ // HARVEST TIME //================================================ function getHerbalismHarvestTimeV23() { const speed = getHerbalismSpeedV23(); const time = HerbalismSystemV23 .config .baseHarvestTime * ( 1 - Math.min( 0.75, speed * 0.01 ) ); return Math.max( HerbalismSystemV23 .config .minHarvestTime, Math.min( HerbalismSystemV23 .config .maxHarvestTime, time ) ); } //================================================ // START HARVEST //================================================ function startHerbalismV23(patchId) { const patch = getHerbPatchV23(patchId); if (!patch) { HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .failedHarvests++; return { success: false, reason: "patch_not_found" }; } updateHerbPatchV23(patch); if (patch.depleted) { return { success: false, reason: "patch_depleted", respawnAt: patch.respawnAt }; } if ( getHerbalismLevelV23() < patch.requiredLevel ) { return { success: false, reason: "level_too_low" }; } if ( HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .has(patchId) ) { return { success: false, reason: "already_harvesting" }; } const herb = selectHerbV23(patch); if (!herb) { return { success: false, reason: "no_available_herb" }; } const now = Date.now(); const duration = getHerbalismHarvestTimeV23(); const session = { patchId, herbId: herb.id, startedAt: now, finishAt: now + duration, duration, completed: false }; HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .set( patchId, session ); HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .attempts++; return { success: true, patchId, herbId: herb.id, duration, finishAt: session.finishAt }; } //================================================ // UPDATE HARVEST //================================================ function updateHerbalismV23(patchId) { const session = HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .get(patchId); if (!session) { return null; } const remaining = Math.max( 0, session.finishAt - Date.now() ); if (remaining <= 0) { session.completed = true; } return { ...session, remaining, ready: remaining <= 0 }; } //================================================ // COMPLETE HARVEST //================================================ function completeHerbalismV23(patchId) { const session = HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .get(patchId); if (!session) { return { success: false, reason: "no_active_session" }; } if ( Date.now() < session.finishAt ) { return { success: false, reason: "harvest_not_finished", remaining: session.finishAt - Date.now() }; } const patch = getHerbPatchV23(patchId); const herb = getHerbV23( session.herbId ); HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .delete(patchId); if (!patch || !herb) { HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .failedHarvests++; return { success: false, reason: "harvest_data_missing" }; } const luck = getHerbalismLuckV23(); const criticalChance = Math.min( 0.5, HerbalismSystemV23 .config .criticalChance + luck * 0.003 ); const critical = Math.random() < criticalChance; let amount = herb.minAmount + Math.floor( Math.random() * ( herb.maxAmount - herb.minAmount + 1 ) ); if (critical) { amount *= HerbalismSystemV23 .config .criticalMultiplier; HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .criticalHarvests++; } if ( typeof addInventoryItemV9 === "function" ) { addInventoryItemV9( herb.id, amount ); } const player = getHerbalismPlayerV23(); const gainedExp = critical ? Math.floor( herb.exp * 1.5 ) : herb.exp; if (player) { if ( typeof player.exp === "number" ) { player.exp += gainedExp; } if ( typeof player.experience === "number" ) { player.experience += gainedExp; } if ( typeof player.herbalismExp === "number" ) { player.herbalismExp += gainedExp; } } patch.harvestsLeft = Math.max( 0, patch.harvestsLeft - 1 ); if ( patch.harvestsLeft <= 0 ) { patch.depleted = true; patch.respawnAt = Date.now() + patch.respawnTime; } HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .successfulHarvests++; HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .totalHerbs += amount; HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .totalValue += herb.value * amount; HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .totalExp += gainedExp; if ( herb.rarity !== HerbRarityV23.COMMON ) { HerbalismSystemV23 .statistics .rareHerbs++; } return { success: true, herbId: herb.id, herbName: herb.name, type: herb.type, rarity: herb.rarity, amount, critical, exp: gainedExp, value: herb.value * amount, effect: herb.effect, harvestsLeft: patch.harvestsLeft, maxHarvests: patch.maxHarvests, depleted: patch.depleted, respawnAt: patch.respawnAt }; } //================================================ // CANCEL HARVEST //================================================ function cancelHerbalismV23(patchId) { if ( !HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .has(patchId) ) { return false; } HerbalismSystemV23 .activeSessions .delete(patchId); return true; } //================================================ // FIND NEAREST PATCH //================================================ function findNearestHerbPatchV23( x, y, maxDistance = 150 ) { let nearest = null; const distance = Math.sqrt( dx * dx + dy * dy ); if ( distance <= maxDistance && distance < nearestDistance ) { nearest = patch; nearestDistance = distance; } } return nearest; } //================================================ // DEFAULT HERBS //================================================ registerHerbV23({ id: "green_leaf", name: "Green Leaf", type: HerbTypeV23.HEALING, rarity: HerbRarityV23.COMMON, minAmount: 1, maxAmount: 3, value: 8, exp: 5, requiredLevel: 1, effect: { hpRestore: 5 } }); //================================================ // ALCHEMY RARITY //================================================ const AlchemyRarityV24 = { COMMON: "common", UNCOMMON: "uncommon", RARE: "rare", EPIC: "epic", LEGENDARY: "legendary", MYTHIC: "mythic" }; //================================================ // ALCHEMY RECIPE CLASS //================================================ class AlchemyRecipeV24 { constructor(config = {}) { this.id = config.id || "recipe_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2); } } //================================================ // REGISTER RECIPE //================================================ function registerAlchemyRecipeV24(config) { const recipe = config instanceof AlchemyRecipeV24 ? config : new AlchemyRecipeV24(config); } //================================================ // START BREW //================================================ function startAlchemyBrewV24(recipeId) { const recipe = getAlchemyRecipeV24(recipeId); //================================================ // WARRIOR RUNTIME DATA //================================================ function ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ) { if (!player) { return null; } if (!player.warrior) { const stats = WarriorVitalSystemV27 .defaults; if (!warrior) { return false; } warrior[statName] = Number(value) || 0; if ( Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call( player, statName ) ) { player[statName] = warrior[statName]; } return true; } //================================================ // SYNC WARRIOR STATS //================================================ function syncWarriorStatsV27( player ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); player.attack = warrior.attack; player.defense = warrior.defense; player.alive = warrior.alive; } //================================================ // GET PLAYER CENTER //================================================ function getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ) { if (!player) { return { x: 0, y: 0 }; } const x = Number(player.x) || 0; const y = Number(player.y) || 0; const width = Number(player.width) || 0; const height = Number(player.height) || 0; return { x: x + width / 2, y: y + height / 2 }; } //================================================ // GET WARRIOR FACING VECTOR //================================================ function getWarriorFacingVectorV27( direction = WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction ) { switch (direction) { case WarriorDirectionV26.UP: return { x: 0, y: -1 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.DOWN: return { x: 0, y: 1 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.LEFT: return { x: -1, y: 0 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.RIGHT: return { x: 1, y: 0 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.UP_LEFT: return { x: -0.707, y: -0.707 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.UP_RIGHT: return { x: 0.707, y: -0.707 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.DOWN_LEFT: return { x: -0.707, y: 0.707 }; case WarriorDirectionV26.DOWN_RIGHT: return { x: 0.707, y: 0.707 }; default: return { x: 0, y: 1 }; } } //================================================ // CREATE ATTACK HITBOX //================================================ function createWarriorAttackHitboxV27( player, comboIndex = WarriorSpriteSystemV26.comboIndex ) { const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); const direction = WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction; const vector = getWarriorFacingVectorV27( direction ); const combo = Math.max( 1, comboIndex ); const range = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .baseRange + combo * 8; const width = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .baseWidth + combo * 8; const height = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .baseHeight; const attackCenterX = center.x + vector.x * range; const attackCenterY = center.y + vector.y * range; return { id: "warrior_attack_" + WarriorCombatSystemV27 .attackSerial, owner: player, combo, direction, centerX: attackCenterX, centerY: attackCenterY, x: attackCenterX - width / 2, y: attackCenterY - height / 2, width, height, range, vectorX: vector.x, vectorY: vector.y, active: true, createdAt: Date.now() }; } //================================================ // RECTANGLE COLLISION //================================================ function warriorRectIntersectsV27( a, b ) { if ( !a || !b ) { return false; } return ( a.x < b.x + b.width && a.x + a.width > b.x && a.y < b.y + b.height && a.y + a.height > b.y ); } //================================================ // GET ENTITY HITBOX //================================================ function getWarriorEntityHitboxV27( entity ) { if (!entity) { return null; } //================================================ // GET DAMAGE //================================================ function calculateWarriorDamageV27( player, target, comboIndex = 1 ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); if (!warrior) { return null; } const baseAttack = Math.max( 1, Number(warrior.attack) || 1 ); const comboMultiplier = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .comboMultipliers[ comboIndex ] || 1; const randomMultiplier = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16; const targetDefense = Math.max( 0, Number( target?.defense ) || 0 ); const defenseReduction = 100 / ( 100 + targetDefense ); const critical = Math.random() < Math.max( 0, Math.min( 1, Number( warrior.criticalChance ) || 0 ) ); const criticalMultiplier = critical ? Math.max( 1, Number( warrior.criticalDamage ) || WarriorCombatSystemV27 .criticalMultiplier ) : 1; const rawDamage = baseAttack * comboMultiplier * randomMultiplier * criticalMultiplier; const damage = Math.max( 1, Math.floor( rawDamage * defenseReduction ) ); return { damage, critical, combo: comboIndex, rawDamage, defenseReduction }; } //================================================ // CREATE DAMAGE NUMBER //================================================ function createWarriorDamageNumberV27( x, y, amount, options = {} ) { if ( !WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .enabled ) { return; } if ( WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers.length >= WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .maxNumbers ) { WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers .shift(); } WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers .push({ x, y, amount: Math.max( 0, Math.floor( Number(amount) || 0 ) ), critical: !!options.critical, heal: !!options.heal, life: WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .duration, maxLife: WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .duration, velocityY: options.critical ? - WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .criticalRiseSpeed : - WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .riseSpeed }); } //================================================ // UPDATE DAMAGE NUMBERS //================================================ function updateWarriorDamageNumbersV27( deltaTime ) { const delta = Math.max( 0, Number(deltaTime) || 0 ); for ( let index = WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers.length - 1; index >= 0; index-- ) { const number = WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers[index]; number.life -= delta; number.y += number.velocityY * delta / 1000; if ( number.life <= 0 ) { WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers .splice( index, 1 ); } } } //================================================ // DRAW DAMAGE NUMBERS //================================================ function drawWarriorDamageNumbersV27( context, cameraX = 0, cameraY = 0 ) { if (!context) { return; } context.save(); context.textAlign = "center"; context.textBaseline = "middle"; for ( const number of WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .numbers ) { const alpha = Math.max( 0, Math.min( 1, number.life / number.maxLife ) ); context.globalAlpha = alpha; context.font = ( number.critical ? WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .criticalFontSize : WarriorDamageNumberSystemV27 .config .fontSize ) + "px monospace"; context.fillStyle = number.heal ? "#65ff8a" : number.critical ? "#ffd54a" : "#ffffff"; context.strokeStyle = "#1a1010"; context.lineWidth = number.critical ? 4 : 3; const screenX = number.x - cameraX; const screenY = number.y - cameraY; const text = ( number.critical ? "CRIT " : "" ) + number.amount; context.strokeText( text, screenX, screenY ); context.fillText( text, screenX, screenY ); } context.restore(); } //================================================ // CREATE SLASH EFFECT //================================================ function createWarriorSlashEffectV27( player, comboIndex ) { if ( !WarriorEffectSystemV27 .enabled ) { return; } if ( WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects.length >= WarriorEffectSystemV27 .maxEffects ) { WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects.shift(); } const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects .push({ type: "slash", x: center.x, y: center.y, direction: WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction, combo: comboIndex, life: WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .slashDuration, maxLife: WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .slashDuration, radius: 48 + comboIndex * 10 }); } //================================================ // CREATE HIT EFFECT //================================================ function createWarriorHitEffectV27( x, y, critical = false ) { if ( WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects.length >= WarriorEffectSystemV27 .maxEffects ) { WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects.shift(); } WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects .push({ type: critical ? "critical" : "hit", x, y, life: critical ? WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .criticalDuration : WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .hitDuration, maxLife: critical ? WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .criticalDuration : WarriorEffectSystemV27 .config .hitDuration, radius: critical ? 32 : 20 }); } //================================================ // UPDATE EFFECTS //================================================ function updateWarriorEffectsV27( deltaTime ) { const delta = Math.max( 0, Number(deltaTime) || 0 ); for ( let index = WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects.length - 1; index >= 0; index-- ) { const effect = WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects[index]; effect.life -= delta; if ( effect.life <= 0 ) { WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects .splice( index, 1 ); } } } //================================================ // DRAW EFFECTS //================================================ function drawWarriorEffectsV27( context, cameraX = 0, cameraY = 0 ) { if (!context) { return; } context.save(); for ( const effect of WarriorEffectSystemV27 .effects ) { const progress = 1 - Math.max( 0, effect.life ) / effect.maxLife; const alpha = Math.max( 0, 1 - progress ); const x = effect.x - cameraX; const y = effect.y - cameraY; context.globalAlpha = alpha; if ( effect.type === "slash" ) { const vector = getWarriorFacingVectorV27( effect.direction ); const radius = effect.radius * ( 0.7 + progress * 0.5 ); context.strokeStyle = effect.combo === 3 ? } const damage = Math.max( 1, Number( damageData.damage ) || 1 ); if ( typeof target.takeDamage === "function" ) { target.takeDamage( damage, player, damageData ); } else if ( Number.isFinite( target.health ) ) { target.health -= damage; if ( target.health <= 0 ) { target.health = 0; target.dead = true; target.isDead = true; } } const targetBox = getWarriorEntityHitboxV27( target ); const targetX = targetBox ? targetBox.x + targetBox.width / 2 : Number(target.x) || 0; } const power = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .knockbackPower * multiplier; if ( Number.isFinite( target.velocityX ) ) { target.velocityX += vectorX * power; } else if ( Number.isFinite( target.vx ) ) { target.vx += vectorX * power; } //================================================ // RESOLVE ATTACK AGAINST TARGETS //================================================ function resolveWarriorAttackV27( player, targets = [] ) { const attackHitbox = WarriorCombatSystemV27 .attacks .get( WarriorCombatSystemV27 .activeAttackId ); const skill = getWarriorSkillV28( skillId ); const skillRuntime = getPlayerWarriorSkillV28( player, skillId ); if ( !warrior || !skill || !skillRuntime ) { return false; } if ( !warrior.alive ) { return false; } if ( !skillRuntime.unlocked ) { return false; } if ( skillRuntime.cooldownRemaining > 0 ) { return false; } if ( warrior.mana < skill.manaCost ) { return false; } if ( warrior.stamina < skill.staminaCost ) { return false; } return true; } //================================================ // SPEND SKILL COST //================================================ function spendWarriorSkillCostV28( player, skill ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); warrior.mana = Math.max( 0, warrior.mana ); warrior.stamina = Math.max( 0, warrior.stamina ); syncWarriorStatsV27( player ); return true; } //================================================ // CREATE SKILL EFFECT //================================================ function createWarriorSkillEffectV28( type, data = {} ) { if ( WarriorSkillSystemV28 .effects.length >= WarriorSkillSystemV28 .config .maxEffects ) { WarriorSkillSystemV28 .effects .shift(); } const effect = { type, x: Number(data.x) || 0, y: Number(data.y) || 0, radius: Number(data.radius) || 30, direction: data.direction || WarriorDirectionV26.DOWN, life: Number(data.life) || 300, maxLife: Number(data.life) || 300, rotation: Number(data.rotation) || 0, rotationSpeed: Number(data.rotationSpeed) || 0, owner: data.owner || null }; WarriorSkillSystemV28 .effects .push(effect); return effect; } //================================================ // GET SKILL LEVEL MULTIPLIER //================================================ function getWarriorSkillLevelMultiplierV28( player, skillId ) { const runtime = getPlayerWarriorSkillV28( player, skillId ); if (!runtime) { return 1; } const level = Math.max( 1, Number(runtime.level) || 1 ); return ( 1 + ( level - 1 ) * 0.15 ); } //================================================ // DAMAGE TARGET WITH SKILL //================================================ function damageTargetWithWarriorSkillV28( player, target, skill, multiplier, knockback, directionVector ) { if ( !target || target.dead || target.isDead || target.alive === false ) { return false; } const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); if (!warrior) { return false; } const targetDefense = Math.max( 0, Number(target.defense) || 0 ); const critical = Math.random() < Math.max( 0, Math.min( 1, warrior.criticalChance ) ); const criticalMultiplier = critical ? warrior.criticalDamage : 1; const damage = Math.max( 1, Math.floor( warrior.attack * multiplier * criticalMultiplier * ( 100 / ( 100 + targetDefense ) ) ) ); if ( typeof target.takeDamage === "function" ) { target.takeDamage( damage, player, { skill: skill.id, critical } ); } else if ( Number.isFinite( target.health ) ) { target.health = Math.max( 0, target.health - damage ); if ( target.health <= 0 ) { target.dead = true; target.isDead = true; } } const box = getWarriorEntityHitboxV27( target ); const centerX = box.x + box.width / 2; const centerY = box.y + box.height / 2; createWarriorDamageNumberV27( centerX, centerY - 12, damage, { critical } ); createWarriorHitEffectV27( centerX, centerY, critical ); applyWarriorKnockbackV27( target, directionVector.x, directionVector.y, knockback / WarriorCombatSystemV27 .knockbackPower ); warrior.damageDealt += damage; if (critical) { warrior.criticalHits++; } return true; } //================================================ // GET TARGETS IN FRONT //================================================ function getWarriorTargetsInFrontV28( player, targets, range, width ) { const result = []; const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); const vector = getWarriorFacingVectorV27( WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction ); const hitbox = { x: center.x + vector.x * range - width / 2, y: center.y + vector.y * range - width / 2, width, height: width }; if ( warriorRectIntersectsV27( hitbox, targetBox ) ) { result.push(target); } } return result; } //================================================ // GET TARGETS IN RADIUS //================================================ function getWarriorTargetsInRadiusV28( player, targets, radius ) { const result = []; const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); const radiusSquared = radius * radius; for ( const target of targets ) { if ( !target || target === player ) { continue; } if ( target.dead || target.isDead || target.alive === false ) { continue; } const box = getWarriorEntityHitboxV27( target ); const targetX = box.x + box.width / 2; const targetY = box.y + box.height / 2; const dx = targetX - center.x; const dy = targetY - center.y; const distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy; if ( distanceSquared <= radiusSquared ) { result.push(target); } } return result; } //================================================ // CAST POWER SLASH //================================================ function castPowerSlashV28( player, skill, targets ) { const levelMultiplier = getWarriorSkillLevelMultiplierV28( player, skill.id ); const vector = getWarriorFacingVectorV27( WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction ); const hitTargets = getWarriorTargetsInFrontV28( player, targets, skill.range, skill.radius ); for ( const target of hitTargets ) { damageTargetWithWarriorSkillV28( player, target, skill, skill.damageMultiplier * levelMultiplier, skill.knockback, vector ); } const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); createWarriorSkillEffectV28( "power_slash", { x: center.x + vector.x * 70, y: center.y + vector.y * 70, radius: skill.radius, direction: WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction, life: 340, owner: player } ); return true; } //================================================ // CAST WHIRLWIND //================================================ function castWhirlwindV28( player, skill ) { const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); const active = { id: "active_whirlwind_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random(), skillId: skill.id, type: "whirlwind", owner: player, life: skill.duration, maxLife: skill.duration, hitTimer: 0, hitInterval: 240, hitTargets: new Set(), radius: skill.radius, damageMultiplier: skill.damageMultiplier * getWarriorSkillLevelMultiplierV28( player, skill.id ), knockback: skill.knockback }; WarriorSkillSystemV28 .activeSkills .push(active); createWarriorSkillEffectV28( "whirlwind", { x: center.x, y: center.y, radius: skill.radius, life: skill.duration, rotationSpeed: 12, owner: player } ); const active = { id: "active_wind_charge_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random(), skillId: skill.id, type: "wind_charge", owner: player, life: skill.duration, maxLife: skill.duration, vectorX: vector.x, vectorY: vector.y, speed: skill.range / ( skill.duration / 1000 ), radius: skill.radius, hitTargets: new Set(), damageMultiplier: skill.damageMultiplier * getWarriorSkillLevelMultiplierV28( player, skill.id ), knockback: skill.knockback }; WarriorSkillSystemV28 .activeSkills .push(active); const center = getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); createWarriorSkillEffectV28( "wind_charge", { x: center.x, y: center.y, radius: 52, direction: WarriorSpriteSystemV26 .direction, life: skill.duration, owner: player } ); const active = { id: "active_heroic_storm_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random(), skillId: skill.id, type: "heroic_storm", owner: player, life: skill.duration, maxLife: skill.duration, hitTimer: 0, hitInterval: 260, hitTargets: new Set(), radius: skill.radius, damageMultiplier: skill.damageMultiplier * getWarriorSkillLevelMultiplierV28( player, skill.id ), knockback: skill.knockback, angle: 0 }; } runtime.cooldownRemaining = skill.cooldown; let success = false; switch ( skill.type ) { case "frontal_attack": success = castPowerSlashV28( player, skill, targets ); break; case "area_attack": success = castWhirlwindV28( player, skill ); const upgrade = Math.max( 0, Number(instance.upgrade) || 0 ); const upgradeMultiplier = 1 + upgrade * 0.08; const multiplier = rarity.multiplier * upgradeMultiplier; return { id: base.id, name: base.name, slot: base.slot, rarity: base.rarity, attack: base.attack * multiplier, defense: base.defense * multiplier, maxHealth: base.maxHealth * multiplier, maxMana: base.maxMana * multiplier, maxStamina: base.maxStamina * multiplier, criticalChance: base.criticalChance * multiplier, criticalDamage: base.criticalDamage * multiplier, moveSpeed: base.moveSpeed * multiplier }; //================================================ // ENSURE LIFE RUNTIME //================================================ function ensureWarriorLifeRuntimeV30( player ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); } const reduction = getWarriorDamageReductionV30( warrior ); let finalDamage = Math.max( 1, Math.floor( rawDamage * ( 1 - reduction ) ) ); if ( data.ignoreDefense ) { finalDamage = Math.max( 1, Math.floor( rawDamage ) ); } //================================================ // GET EXPERIENCE REWARD //================================================ function getWarriorExperienceRewardV30( player, enemy ) { if (!enemy) { return 0; } else if ( levelDifference <= -5 ) { multiplier = 0.5; } return Math.max( 1, Math.floor( reward * multiplier ) ); } //================================================ // REWARD EXPERIENCE FOR ENEMY KILL //================================================ function rewardWarriorForKillV30( player, enemy ) { const experience = getWarriorExperienceRewardV30( player, enemy ); case "down": return Math.PI / 2; case "left": return Math.PI; case "right": default: return 0; } } //================================================ // NORMALIZE ANGLE //================================================ function normalizeWarriorAngleV31( angle ) { while ( angle > Math.PI ) { angle -= Math.PI * 2; } while ( angle < -Math.PI ) { angle += Math.PI * 2; } return angle; } //================================================ // GET TARGET CENTER //================================================ function getWarriorTargetCenterV31( target ) { if (!target) { return null; } const width = Number( target.width ) || Number( target.size ) || 32; const height = Number( target.height ) || Number( target.size ) || 32; const x = Number(target.x) || 0; const y = Number(target.y) || 0; return { x: x + width / 2, y: y + height / 2, radius: Math.max( width, height ) * 0.35 }; } //================================================ // GET TARGET ID //================================================ function getWarriorTargetIdV31( target, index = 0 ) { if (!target) { return null; } return ( target.id || target.uuid || target.entityId || target.name || ( "target_" + index ) ); } //================================================ // GET ATTACK ORIGIN //================================================ function getWarriorAttackOriginV31( player ) { return getWarriorPlayerCenterV27( player ); } //================================================ // CHECK TARGET INSIDE ATTACK ARC //================================================ function isTargetInsideWarriorAttackV31( player, target, attack ) { if ( !player || !target || !attack ) { return false; } const origin = getWarriorAttackOriginV31( player ); const center = getWarriorTargetCenterV31( target ); if ( !origin || !center ) { return false; } const dx = center.x - origin.x; const dy = center.y - origin.y; const facing = getWarriorDirectionAngleV31( direction ); const targetAngle = Math.atan2( dy, dx ); const difference = Math.abs( normalizeWarriorAngleV31( targetAngle - facing ) ); return ( difference <= attack.arc / 2 ); } //================================================ // GET WARRIOR ATTACK DAMAGE //================================================ function getWarriorAttackDamageV31( player, attack ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); if ( Math.random() < criticalChance ) { critical = true; } let damage = baseAttack * multiplier; if (critical) { const criticalDamage = Math.max( 1.25, Number( warrior.criticalDamage ) || 1.5 ); damage *= criticalDamage; } return { damage: Math.max( 1, Math.floor( damage ) ), critical }; } //================================================ // APPLY DAMAGE TO TARGET //================================================ function applyWarriorDamageToTargetV31( player, target, damageData, attack ) { if ( !target || !damageData ) { return 0; } const damage = Math.max( 1, Number( damageData.damage ) || 1 ); let applied = false; if ( typeof target.takeDamage === "function" ) { target.takeDamage( damage, player, { critical: damageData.critical, attackId: attack.id } ); applied = true; } else if ( typeof target.damage === "function" ) { target.damage( damage, player ); applied = true; } else if ( Number.isFinite( target.health ) ) { target.health = Math.max( 0, target.health - damage ); } const origin = getWarriorAttackOriginV31( player ); const center = getWarriorTargetCenterV31( target ); if ( !origin || !center ) { return; } const dx = center.x - origin.x; const dy = center.y - origin.y; const length = Math.sqrt( dx * dx + dy * dy ) || 1; const nx = dx / length; const ny = dy / length; if ( Number.isFinite( target.x ) ) { target.x += nx * power; } if ( Number.isFinite( target.y ) ) { target.y += ny * power; } target.knockbackX = nx * power; target.knockbackY = ny * power; } //================================================ // START NORMAL ATTACK //================================================ function startWarriorAdvancedAttackV31( player ) { const warrior = ensureWarriorRuntimeV27( player ); //================================================ // NPC CLASS V33 //================================================ class FriendlyNPCV33 { constructor( definition, options = {} ) { const data = definition || {}; this.id = options.id || data.id || ( "npc_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2) ); this.definitionId = data.id || this.id; this.name = options.name || data.name || "NPC"; this.shortName = options.shortName || data.shortName || this.name; this.category = options.category || data.category || "friendly"; this.role = options.role || data.role || "Friendly NPC"; this.x = Number(options.x) || 0; this.y = Number(options.y) || 0; this.width = Number(options.width) || data.appearance?.width || NPCSystemV33.defaultWidth; this.height = Number(options.height) || data.appearance?.height || NPCSystemV33.defaultHeight; this.direction = options.direction || data.appearance?.direction || "down"; this.scale = Number(options.scale) || data.appearance?.scale || 1; this.spriteKey = options.spriteKey || data.appearance?.spriteKey || null; this.sprite = options.sprite || null; this.visible = options.visible !== false; this.active = options.active !== false; this.friendly = true; this.interactable = options.interactable !== false; this.interactionRange = Number(options.interactionRange) || NPCSystemV33.interactionRange; this.questNPC = options.questNPC === true; this.services = Array.isArray( options.services ) ? options.services : []; this.tags = Array.isArray( data.tags ) ? [ ...data.tags ] : []; this.dialogues = Array.isArray( options.dialogues ) ? [ ...options.dialogues ] : []; this.questIds = Array.isArray( options.questIds ) ? [ ...options.questIds ] : []; this.questStatus = "none"; this.showQuestIcon = options.showQuestIcon !== false; this.customData = options.customData || {}; this.state = { idle: true, moving: false, talking: false, interacting: false, selected: false, nearby: false }; this.animation = { frame: 0, timer: 0, frameDuration: 180, idleOffset: 0, bobTimer: 0 }; } } //================================================ // CREATE NPC FROM DATABASE //================================================ function createNPCV33( definitionId, options = {} ) { const definition = NPCVisualDatabaseV33[ definitionId ]; if (!definition) { console.warn( "NPC definition not found:", definitionId ); return null; } const npc = new FriendlyNPCV33( definition, options ); return npc; } //================================================ // REGISTER NPC //================================================ function registerNPCV33( npc ) { if (!npc) { return null; } if ( NPCSystemV33.npcById[ npc.id ] ) { unregisterNPCV33( npc.id ); } NPCSystemV33.npcs.push( npc ); NPCSystemV33.npcById[ npc.id ] = npc; return npc; } //================================================ // CREATE AND REGISTER NPC //================================================ function spawnNPCV33( definitionId, options = {} ) { const npc = createNPCV33( definitionId, options ); if (!npc) { return null; } return registerNPCV33( npc ); } //================================================ // UNREGISTER NPC //================================================ function unregisterNPCV33( npcId ) { const npc = NPCSystemV33.npcById[ npcId ]; if (!npc) { return false; } const index = NPCSystemV33.npcs .indexOf(npc); if ( index >= 0 ) { NPCSystemV33.npcs.splice( index, 1 ); } delete NPCSystemV33.npcById[ npcId ]; if ( NPCSystemV33.activeNPC === npc ) { NPCSystemV33.activeNPC = null; } if ( NPCSystemV33.nearbyNPC === npc ) { NPCSystemV33.nearbyNPC = null; } return true; } //================================================ // GET NPC //================================================ function getNPCV33( npcId ) { return ( NPCSystemV33.npcById[ npcId ] || null ); } //================================================ // GET ALL NPCS //================================================ function getAllNPCsV33() { return [ ...NPCSystemV33.npcs ]; } //================================================ // GET PLAYER CENTER FOR NPC SYSTEM //================================================ function getNPCPlayerCenterV33( player ) { if (!player) { return null; } return { x: ( Number(player.x) || 0 ) + ( Number(player.width) || 32 ) / 2, y: ( Number(player.y) || 0 ) + ( Number(player.height) || 48 ) / 2 }; } //================================================ // GET NPC CENTER //================================================ function getNPCCenterV33( npc ) { if (!npc) { return null; } return { x: npc.x + npc.width / 2, y: npc.y + npc.height / 2 }; } //================================================ // GET DISTANCE PLAYER TO NPC //================================================ function getPlayerNPCDistanceV33( player, npc ) { const playerCenter = getNPCPlayerCenterV33( player ); const npcCenter = getNPCCenterV33( npc ); if ( !playerCenter || !npcCenter ) { return Infinity; } const dx = npcCenter.x - playerCenter.x; const dy = npcCenter.y - playerCenter.y; return Math.sqrt( dx * dx + dy * dy ); } //================================================ // FIND NEAREST INTERACTABLE NPC //================================================ function findNearestNPCV33( player, maxRange = NPCSystemV33.interactionRange ) { if (!player) { return null; } let nearest = null; let nearestDistance = Infinity; for ( const npc of NPCSystemV33.npcs ) { if ( !npc || !npc.active || !npc.visible || !npc.interactable ) { continue; } const distance = getPlayerNPCDistanceV33( player, npc ); const range = Math.max( maxRange, npc.interactionRange ); if ( distance > range ) { continue; } if ( distance < nearestDistance ) { nearest = npc; nearestDistance = distance; } } return nearest; } //================================================ // SET ACTIVE NPC //================================================ function setActiveNPCV33( npc ) { if ( NPCSystemV33.activeNPC && NPCSystemV33.activeNPC !== npc ) { NPCSystemV33.activeNPC .state .selected = false; NPCSystemV33.activeNPC .state .talking = false; NPCSystemV33.activeNPC .state .interacting = false; } NPCSystemV33.activeNPC = npc || null; if (npc) { npc.state.selected = true; npc.state.talking = true; npc.state.interacting = true; } return npc; } //================================================ // CLOSE ACTIVE NPC //================================================ function closeActiveNPCV33() { const npc = NPCSystemV33.activeNPC; if (npc) { npc.state.selected = false; npc.state.talking = false; npc.state.interacting = false; } NPCSystemV33.activeNPC = null; } //================================================ // INTERACT WITH NPC //================================================ function interactWithNPCV33( player, npc = null ) { if (!player) { return null; } const target = npc || findNearestNPCV33( player ); if (!target) { return null; } if ( !target.active || !target.visible || !target.interactable ) { return null; } setActiveNPCV33( target ); if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.onNPCInteractV33 === "function" ) { window.onNPCInteractV33( target, player ); } return target; } //================================================ // UPDATE NPC ANIMATION //================================================ function updateNPCAnimationV33( npc, deltaTime ) { if (!npc) { return; } npc.animation.timer += deltaTime; npc.animation.bobTimer += deltaTime; if ( npc.animation.timer >= npc.animation.frameDuration ) { npc.animation.timer = 0; npc.animation.frame = ( npc.animation.frame + 1 ) % 2; } npc.animation.idleOffset = Math.sin( npc.animation.bobTimer / 500 ) * 0.5; } //================================================ // UPDATE NPC NEARBY STATE //================================================ function updateNPCNearbyStateV33( player ) { const nearby = findNearestNPCV33( player ); NPCSystemV33.nearbyNPC = nearby; for ( const npc of NPCSystemV33.npcs ) { npc.state.nearby = npc === nearby; } return nearby; } //================================================ // UPDATE ALL NPCS //================================================ function updateNPCSystemV33( player, input, deltaTime ) { if ( !Number.isFinite( deltaTime ) ) { deltaTime = 16; } NPCSystemV33.playerInteractionCooldown = Math.max( 0, NPCSystemV33 .playerInteractionCooldown - deltaTime ); for ( const npc of NPCSystemV33.npcs ) { if ( !npc || !npc.active ) { continue; } updateNPCAnimationV33( npc, deltaTime ); } const nearby = updateNPCNearbyStateV33( player ); const interactPressed = !!( input?.interactPressed || input?.interact || input?.keyEPressed || input?.actionPressed ); if ( interactPressed && nearby && NPCSystemV33 .playerInteractionCooldown <= 0 ) { interactWithNPCV33( player, nearby ); NPCSystemV33 .playerInteractionCooldown = 250; } } //================================================ // DRAW NPC FALLBACK PIXEL BODY // USED UNTIL REAL NPC SPRITES ARE LOADED //================================================ function drawNPCFallbackV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ) { const w = npc.width * npc.scale; const h = npc.height * npc.scale; const x = screenX + ( npc.width - w ) / 2; const y = screenY + ( npc.height - h ) + npc.animation.idleOffset; context.save(); context.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; context.fillStyle = "#2a1c17"; context.fillRect( x + w * 0.22, y, w * 0.56, h * 0.28 ); context.fillStyle = "#f2bf91"; context.fillRect( x + w * 0.27, y + h * 0.15, w * 0.46, h * 0.24 ); context.fillStyle = "#3f4b5f"; context.fillRect( x + w * 0.18, y + h * 0.38, w * 0.64, h * 0.37 ); context.fillStyle = "#23262d"; context.fillRect( x + w * 0.22, y + h * 0.75, w * 0.2, h * 0.22 ); context.fillRect( x + w * 0.58, y + h * 0.75, w * 0.2, h * 0.22 ); context.restore(); } //================================================ // DRAW NPC //================================================ function drawNPCV33( context, npc, cameraX = 0, cameraY = 0 ) { if ( !context || !npc || !npc.visible ) { return; } const screenX = npc.x - cameraX; const screenY = npc.y - cameraY; const sprite = npc.sprite; if ( sprite && sprite.complete ) { const width = npc.width * npc.scale; const height = npc.height * npc.scale; context.save(); context.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; context.drawImage( sprite, screenX + ( npc.width - width ) / 2, screenY + ( npc.height - height ) + npc.animation.idleOffset, width, height ); context.restore(); } else { drawNPCFallbackV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ); } drawNPCNameV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ); drawNPCInteractionHintV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ); } //================================================ // DRAW NPC NAME //================================================ function drawNPCNameV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ) { const text = npc.shortName || npc.name; context.save(); context.font = "bold 12px sans-serif"; context.textAlign = "center"; context.textBaseline = "middle"; const x = screenX + npc.width / 2; const y = screenY - 9; context.lineWidth = 3; context.strokeStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.75)"; context.strokeText( text, x, y ); context.fillStyle = npc.questNPC ? "#ffd35c" : "#ffffff"; context.fillText( text, x, y ); context.restore(); } //================================================ // DRAW INTERACTION HINT //================================================ function drawNPCInteractionHintV33( context, npc, screenX, screenY ) { if ( !npc.state.nearby ) { return; } const x = screenX + npc.width / 2; const y = screenY - 28; context.save(); context.textAlign = "center"; context.textBaseline = "middle"; context.font = "bold 12px sans-serif"; context.fillStyle = "rgba(15,15,20,0.9)"; context.fillRect( x - 13, y - 9, 26, 18 ); context.strokeStyle = "#ffd35c"; context.strokeRect( x - 13, y - 9, 26, 18 ); context.fillStyle = "#ffffff"; context.fillText( "E", x, y + 1 ); context.restore(); } //================================================ // DRAW ALL NPCS //================================================ function drawNPCSystemV33( context, cameraX = 0, cameraY = 0 ) { const sorted = [ ...NPCSystemV33.npcs ] .filter( npc => npc && npc.visible ) .sort( (a, b) => ( a.y + a.height ) - ( b.y + b.height ) ); for ( const npc of sorted ) { drawNPCV33( context, npc, cameraX, cameraY ); } } //================================================ // LOAD NPC SPRITE //================================================ function loadNPCSpriteV33( npc, source ) { if ( !npc || !source || typeof Image === "undefined" ) { return null; } const image = new Image(); image.onload = () => { npc.sprite = image; }; image.onerror = () => { console.warn( "NPC sprite load failed:", npc.id, source ); }; image.src = source; return image; } //================================================ // LOAD SPRITES FOR ALL NPCS //================================================ function loadNPCSpritesV33( spriteSources = {} ) { for ( const npc of NPCSystemV33.npcs ) { const source = spriteSources[ npc.spriteKey ] || spriteSources[ npc.definitionId ]; if (source) { loadNPCSpriteV33( npc, source ); } } } //================================================ // SPAWN DEFAULT 29 NPC APPEARANCES //================================================ function createDefaultNPCCollectionV33( positions = {} ) { const ids = Object.keys( NPCVisualDatabaseV33 ); const created = []; for ( const id of ids ) { const position = positions[id] || {}; const npc = spawnNPCV33( id, position ); if (npc) { created.push( npc ); } } return created; } //================================================ // NPC SYSTEM INITIALIZATION //================================================ function initializeNPCSystemV33() { if ( NPCSystemV33.initialized ) { return; } NPCSystemV33.initialized = true; } //================================================ // EXPORT NPC V33 //================================================ if ( typeof window !== "undefined" ) { window.NPCSystemV33 = NPCSystemV33; window.NPCVisualDatabaseV33 = NPCVisualDatabaseV33; window.FriendlyNPCV33 = FriendlyNPCV33; window.initializeNPCSystemV33 = initializeNPCSystemV33; window.createNPCV33 = createNPCV33; window.registerNPCV33 = registerNPCV33; window.spawnNPCV33 = spawnNPCV33; window.unregisterNPCV33 = unregisterNPCV33; window.getNPCV33 = getNPCV33; window.getAllNPCsV33 = getAllNPCsV33; window.findNearestNPCV33 = findNearestNPCV33; window.interactWithNPCV33 = interactWithNPCV33; window.closeActiveNPCV33 = closeActiveNPCV33; window.updateNPCSystemV33 = updateNPCSystemV33; window.drawNPCSystemV33 = drawNPCSystemV33; window.drawNPCV33 = drawNPCV33; window.loadNPCSpriteV33 = loadNPCSpriteV33; window.loadNPCSpritesV33 = loadNPCSpritesV33; window.createDefaultNPCCollectionV33 = createDefaultNPCCollectionV33; } //================================================ // INITIALIZE V33 //================================================ initializeNPCSystemV33(); //================================================ // END NPC CORE SYSTEM V33 // NEXT: V34 NPC DIALOGUE SYSTEM // THEN V35 QUEST NPC SYSTEM //================================================ //================================================ // HERO OF WIND LAND // NPC DIALOGUE SYSTEM V34 // APPEND DIRECTLY AFTER V33 // DO NOT MODIFY PREVIOUS CODE //================================================ //================================================ // DIALOGUE SYSTEM V34 //================================================ const NPCDialogueSystemV34 = { version: 34, initialized: false, active: false, npc: null, pages: [], currentPageIndex: 0, selectedChoiceIndex: 0, typingText: "", fullText: "", typingSpeed: 35, typingTimer: 0, typingComplete: false, autoClose: false, closeTimer: 0, inputCooldown: 0, history: [], dialogueDatabase: Object.create(null), ui: { visible: true, x: 40, y: 0, width: 0, height: 150, padding: 18, portraitSize: 76, titleHeight: 28, choiceHeight: 28, maxChoices: 5 } }; //================================================ // DIALOGUE PAGE CLASS //================================================ class NPCDialoguePageV34 { constructor( options = {} ) { this.id = options.id || ( "dialogue_page_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2) ); this.speaker = options.speaker || ""; this.text = options.text || ""; this.portrait = options.portrait || null; this.choices = Array.isArray( options.choices ) ? options.choices : []; this.next = options.next ?? null; this.close = options.close === true; this.event = options.event || null; this.condition = options.condition || null; this.metadata = options.metadata || {}; } } //================================================ // DIALOGUE CHOICE CLASS //================================================ class NPCDialogueChoiceV34 { constructor( options = {} ) { this.id = options.id || ( "choice_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2) );
