//================================================ // REGISTER DEFAULT DIALOGUES //================================================ function registerDefaultNPCDialoguesV34() { for ( const npcId in DefaultNPCDialoguesV34 ) { NPCDialogueSystemV34 .dialogueDatabase[ npcId ] = DefaultNPCDialoguesV34[ npcId ].map( page => new NPCDialoguePageV34( { ...page, speaker: page.speaker || ( NPCVisualDatabaseV33[ npcId ]?.name || npcId ), choices: ( page.choices || [] ).map( choice => new NPCDialogueChoiceV34( choice ) ) } ) ); } } //================================================ // GET NPC DIALOGUE //================================================ function getNPCDialogueV34( npcOrId ) { const id = typeof npcOrId === "string" ? npcOrId : ( npcOrId?.definitionId || npcOrId?.id ); return ( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .dialogueDatabase[ id ] || [] ); } //================================================ // SET NPC DIALOGUE //================================================ function setNPCDialogueV34( npcId, pages ) { if ( !npcId || !Array.isArray(pages) ) { return false; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .dialogueDatabase[ npcId ] = pages.map( page => page instanceof NPCDialoguePageV34 ? page : new NPCDialoguePageV34( { ...page, choices: ( page.choices || [] ).map( choice => choice instanceof NPCDialogueChoiceV34 ? choice : new NPCDialogueChoiceV34( choice ) ) } ) ); return true; } //================================================ // GET CURRENT DIALOGUE PAGE //================================================ function getCurrentDialoguePageV34() { return ( NPCDialogueSystemV34.pages[ NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex ] || null ); } //================================================ // TEST CONDITION //================================================ function checkDialogueConditionV34( condition, context = {} ) { if (!condition) { return true; } if ( typeof condition === "function" ) { try { return !!condition( context ); } catch (error) { console.warn( "Dialogue condition error:", error ); return false; } } if ( typeof condition === "boolean" ) { return condition; } return true; } //================================================ // FILTER AVAILABLE CHOICES //================================================ function getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, context = {} ) { if ( !page || !Array.isArray( page.choices ) ) { return []; } return page.choices.filter( choice => !choice.disabled && checkDialogueConditionV34( choice.condition, context ) ); } //================================================ // SET CURRENT PAGE //================================================ function setDialoguePageV34( index ) { if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34.active ) { return false; } if ( index < 0 || index >= NPCDialogueSystemV34 .pages.length ) { return false; } const page = NPCDialogueSystemV34.pages[ index ]; if ( !checkDialogueConditionV34( page.condition, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc } ) ) { return false; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex = index; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .fullText = page.text || ""; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText = ""; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingTimer = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete = false; if ( page.event ) { triggerDialogueEventV34( page.event, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc, page } ); } return true; } //================================================ // OPEN NPC DIALOGUE //================================================ function openNPCDialogueV34( npc, pages = null ) { if (!npc) { return false; } const dialoguePages = pages || getNPCDialogueV34( npc ); if ( !dialoguePages || dialoguePages.length === 0 ) { return false; } NPCDialogueSystemV34.active = true; NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc = npc; NPCDialogueSystemV34.pages = dialoguePages; NPCDialogueSystemV34.history = []; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 120; if ( typeof setActiveNPCV33 === "function" ) { setActiveNPCV33( npc ); } setDialoguePageV34( 0 ); return true; } //================================================ // CLOSE DIALOGUE //================================================ function closeNPCDialogueV34() { const npc = NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc; NPCDialogueSystemV34.active = false; NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc = null; NPCDialogueSystemV34.pages = []; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex = 0; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText = ""; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .fullText = ""; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete = false; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 180; if ( npc && typeof closeActiveNPCV33 === "function" ) { closeActiveNPCV33(); } return true; } //================================================ // COMPLETE TYPING IMMEDIATELY //================================================ function completeDialogueTypingV34() { NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText = NPCDialogueSystemV34 .fullText; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete = true; } //================================================ // GO TO NEXT DIALOGUE //================================================ function nextDialoguePageV34() { if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34.active ) { return false; } if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete ) { completeDialogueTypingV34(); return true; } const page = getCurrentDialoguePageV34(); if (!page) { closeNPCDialogueV34(); return false; } const choices = getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc } ); if ( choices.length > 0 ) { return false; } if ( page.close ) { closeNPCDialogueV34(); return true; } if ( typeof page.next === "number" ) { NPCDialogueSystemV34 .history.push( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex ); return setDialoguePageV34( page.next ); } const nextIndex = NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex + 1; if ( nextIndex >= NPCDialogueSystemV34 .pages.length ) { closeNPCDialogueV34(); return true; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .history.push( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex ); return setDialoguePageV34( nextIndex ); } //================================================ // SELECT DIALOGUE CHOICE //================================================ function selectDialogueChoiceV34( index, context = {} ) { const page = getCurrentDialoguePageV34(); if (!page) { return false; } if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete ) { completeDialogueTypingV34(); return true; } const choices = getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc, ...context } ); const choice = choices[index]; if (!choice) { return false; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex = index; if ( choice.event ) { triggerDialogueEventV34( choice.event, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc, page, choice, ...context } ); } if ( choice.close ) { closeNPCDialogueV34(); return true; } if ( typeof choice.next === "number" ) { NPCDialogueSystemV34 .history.push( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .currentPageIndex ); return setDialoguePageV34( choice.next ); } return nextDialoguePageV34(); } //================================================ // DIALOGUE EVENT DISPATCHER //================================================ function triggerDialogueEventV34( eventName, context = {} ) { if (!eventName) { return; } if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.onNPCDialogueEventV34 === "function" ) { window.onNPCDialogueEventV34( eventName, context ); } if ( typeof window !== "undefined" ) { const handlerName = "onDialogueEvent_" + eventName; if ( typeof window[ handlerName ] === "function" ) { window[ handlerName ]( context ); } } } //================================================ // MOVE CHOICE SELECTION //================================================ function moveDialogueChoiceV34( direction ) { const page = getCurrentDialoguePageV34(); if (!page) { return; } const choices = getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc } ); if ( choices.length === 0 ) { return; } let index = NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex + direction; if ( index < 0 ) { index = choices.length - 1; } if ( index >= choices.length ) { index = 0; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex = index; } //================================================ // UPDATE TYPEWRITER //================================================ function updateDialogueTypingV34( deltaTime ) { if ( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete ) { return; } const text = NPCDialogueSystemV34 .fullText; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingTimer += deltaTime; const charsToShow = Math.floor( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingTimer / NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingSpeed ); if ( charsToShow >= text.length ) { NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText = text; NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete = true; return; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText = text.slice( 0, charsToShow ); } //================================================ // UPDATE DIALOGUE INPUT //================================================ function updateNPCDialogueInputV34( input = {} ) { if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34.active ) { return; } if ( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown > 0 ) { return; } if ( input.upPressed || input.keyWPressed || input.arrowUpPressed ) { moveDialogueChoiceV34( -1 ); NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 100; return; } if ( input.downPressed || input.keySPressed || input.arrowDownPressed ) { moveDialogueChoiceV34( 1 ); NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 100; return; } if ( input.confirmPressed || input.enterPressed || input.spacePressed || input.interactPressed || input.keyEPressed ) { const page = getCurrentDialoguePageV34(); const choices = getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc } ); if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete ) { completeDialogueTypingV34(); } else if ( choices.length > 0 ) { selectDialogueChoiceV34( NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex ); } else { nextDialoguePageV34(); } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 150; return; } if ( input.cancelPressed || input.escapePressed ) { closeNPCDialogueV34(); NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = 180; } } //================================================ // UPDATE DIALOGUE SYSTEM //================================================ function updateNPCDialogueSystemV34( deltaTime, input = {} ) { if ( !Number.isFinite( deltaTime ) ) { deltaTime = 16; } NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown = Math.max( 0, NPCDialogueSystemV34 .inputCooldown - deltaTime ); if ( !NPCDialogueSystemV34.active ) { return; } updateDialogueTypingV34( deltaTime ); updateNPCDialogueInputV34( input ); } //================================================ // WORD WRAPPING //================================================ function wrapDialogueTextV34( context, text, maxWidth ) { const words = String(text || "") .split(/\s+/); const lines = []; let line = ""; for ( const word of words ) { const test = line ? line + " " + word : word; if ( context.measureText( test ).width > maxWidth && line ) { lines.push( line ); line = word; } else { line = test; } } if (line) { lines.push( line ); } return lines; } //================================================ // DRAW DIALOGUE PORTRAIT //================================================ function drawDialoguePortraitV34( context, npc, x, y, size ) { context.save(); context.fillStyle = "rgba(20,20,28,0.95)"; context.fillRect( x, y, size, size ); context.strokeStyle = "#c9a64d"; context.lineWidth = 2; context.strokeRect( x, y, size, size ); if ( npc?.sprite && npc.sprite.complete ) { context.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; context.drawImage( npc.sprite, x + 4, y + 4, size - 8, size - 8 ); } else { context.fillStyle = "#9b6b4d"; context.fillRect( x + size * 0.25, y + size * 0.18, size * 0.5, size * 0.55 ); context.fillStyle = "#f2bf91"; context.fillRect( x + size * 0.3, y + size * 0.25, size * 0.4, size * 0.28 ); context.fillStyle = "#3d4d62"; context.fillRect( x + size * 0.2, y + size * 0.55, size * 0.6, size * 0.32 ); } context.restore(); } //================================================ // DRAW DIALOGUE BOX //================================================ function drawNPCDialogueV34( context, canvasWidth, canvasHeight ) { if ( !context || !NPCDialogueSystemV34.active ) { return; } const page = getCurrentDialoguePageV34(); if (!page) { return; } const ui = NPCDialogueSystemV34.ui; const x = ui.x; const width = canvasWidth - ui.x * 2; const choiceList = getAvailableDialogueChoicesV34( page, { npc: NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc } ); const textHeight = choiceList.length > 0 ? 58 : 74; const height = ui.padding * 2 + textHeight + ui.titleHeight + choiceList.length * ui.choiceHeight + ( choiceList.length > 0 ? 8 : 0 ); const y = canvasHeight - height - 28; context.save(); context.fillStyle = "rgba(10,11,16,0.94)"; context.fillRect( x, y, width, height ); context.lineWidth = 2; context.strokeStyle = "#c9a64d"; context.strokeRect( x, y, width, height ); const portraitSize = Math.min( ui.portraitSize, height - 24 ); drawDialoguePortraitV34( context, NPCDialogueSystemV34.npc, x + 12, y + ( height - portraitSize ) / 2, portraitSize ); const textX = x + portraitSize + 30; const textWidth = width - portraitSize - 48; context.font = "bold 18px sans-serif"; context.textAlign = "left"; context.textBaseline = "middle"; context.fillStyle = "#ffd35c"; context.fillText( page.speaker || NPCDialogueSystemV34 .npc?.name || "NPC", textX, y + 24 ); context.font = "16px sans-serif"; context.fillStyle = "#f5f5f5"; const lines = wrapDialogueTextV34( context, NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingText, textWidth ); let textY = y + 52; for ( const line of lines.slice( 0, 3 ) ) { context.fillText( line, textX, textY ); textY += 21; } if ( choiceList.length > 0 && NPCDialogueSystemV34 .typingComplete ) { let choiceY = y + height - choiceList.length * ui.choiceHeight - 12; for ( let i = 0; i < choiceList.length; i++ ) { const selected = i === NPCDialogueSystemV34 .selectedChoiceIndex; } NPCDialogueKeyboardV34 .keys[key] = true; } ); window.addEventListener( "keyup", event => { const key = event.key .toLowerCase(); NPCDialogueKeyboardV34 .keys[key] = false; } ); } //================================================ // GET KEYBOARD DIALOGUE INPUT //================================================ function getNPCDialogueKeyboardInputV34() { const pressed = NPCDialogueKeyboardV34 .pressed; const input = { upPressed: !!( pressed["w"] || pressed["arrowup"] ), downPressed: !!( pressed["s"] || pressed["arrowdown"] ), confirmPressed: !!( pressed["enter"] || pressed[" "] || pressed["e"] ), cancelPressed: !!pressed["escape"] }; //================================================ // END NPC DIALOGUE SYSTEM V34 // NEXT: V35 QUEST NPC SYSTEM //================================================ //================================================ // HERO OF WIND LAND // QUEST NPC SYSTEM V35 // APPEND DIRECTLY AFTER V34 // DO NOT MODIFY PREVIOUS CODE //================================================ //================================================ // QUEST SYSTEM V35 //================================================ const QuestSystemV35 = { version: 35, initialized: false, quests: Object.create(null), activeQuests: [], completedQuests: [], failedQuests: [], trackedQuestId: null, pendingRewards: [], listeners: Object.create(null) }; //================================================ // QUEST STATES //================================================ const QUEST_STATE_V35 = { LOCKED: "locked", AVAILABLE: "available", ACTIVE: "active", READY: "ready", COMPLETED: "completed", FAILED: "failed" }; //================================================ // QUEST TYPES //================================================ const QUEST_OBJECTIVE_TYPE_V35 = { KILL: "kill", COLLECT: "collect", TALK: "talk", VISIT: "visit", CRAFT: "craft", UPGRADE: "upgrade", FISH: "fish", FARM: "farm", MINE: "mine", WIN: "win", EXPLORE: "explore", CUSTOM: "custom" }; //================================================ // QUEST CLASS //================================================ class QuestV35 { constructor( options = {} ) { this.id = options.id || ( "quest_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2) ); this.name = options.name || "Nhiệm vụ chưa đặt tên"; this.description = options.description || ""; this.shortDescription = options.shortDescription || this.description; this.giverNPC = options.giverNPC || null; this.turnInNPC = options.turnInNPC || this.giverNPC; this.category = options.category || "side"; this.level = Number.isFinite( options.level ) ? options.level : 1; this.repeatable = options.repeatable === true; this.hidden = options.hidden === true; this.autoComplete = options.autoComplete === true; this.autoAccept = options.autoAccept === true; this.prerequisites = Array.isArray( options.prerequisites ) ? [ ...options.prerequisites ] : []; this.objectives = Array.isArray( options.objectives ) ? options.objectives.map( objective => new QuestObjectiveV35( objective ) ) : []; this.rewards = options.rewards || {}; this.metadata = options.metadata || {}; this.dialogue = options.dialogue || {}; } } //================================================ // QUEST OBJECTIVE CLASS //================================================ class QuestObjectiveV35 { constructor( options = {} ) { this.id = options.id || ( "objective_" + Date.now() + "_" + Math.random() .toString(36) .slice(2) ); this.type = options.type || QUEST_OBJECTIVE_TYPE_V35 .CUSTOM; this.target = options.target || null; this.name = options.name || ""; this.description = options.description || ""; this.required = Number.isFinite( options.required ) ? Math.max( 1, options.required ) : 1; this.progress = Number.isFinite( options.progress ) ? Math.max( 0, options.progress ) : 0; this.completed = options.completed === true; this.hidden = options.hidden === true; this.metadata = options.metadata || {}; } } //================================================ // QUEST EVENT LISTENER //================================================ function onQuestEventV35( eventName, callback ) { if ( !eventName || typeof callback !== "function" ) { return false; } if ( !QuestSystemV35.listeners[ eventName ] ) { QuestSystemV35.listeners[ eventName ] = []; } QuestSystemV35.listeners[ eventName ].push( callback ); return true; } //================================================ // EMIT QUEST EVENT //================================================ function emitQuestEventV35( eventName, data = {} ) { const listeners = QuestSystemV35.listeners[ eventName ] || []; for ( const callback of listeners ) { try { callback( data ); } catch (error) { console.warn( "Quest event error:", eventName, error ); } } if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.onQuestEventV35 === "function" ) { try { window.onQuestEventV35( eventName, data ); } catch (error) { console.warn( "Global quest event error:", error ); } } } //================================================ // REGISTER QUEST //================================================ function registerQuestV35( questData ) { const quest = questData instanceof QuestV35 ? questData : new QuestV35( questData ); QuestSystemV35.quests[ quest.id ] = quest; return quest; } //================================================ // GET QUEST //================================================ function getQuestV35( questId ) { return ( QuestSystemV35.quests[ questId ] || null ); } //================================================ // GET ALL QUESTS //================================================ function getAllQuestsV35() { return Object.values( QuestSystemV35.quests ); } //================================================ // HAS QUEST ACTIVE //================================================ function hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) { return QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests .includes( questId ); } //================================================ // HAS QUEST COMPLETED //================================================ function hasCompletedQuestV35( questId ) { return QuestSystemV35 .completedQuests .includes( questId ); } //================================================ // CHECK QUEST PREREQUISITES //================================================ function canStartQuestV35( questId ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if (!quest) { return false; } if ( hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } if ( hasCompletedQuestV35( questId ) && !quest.repeatable ) { return false; } for ( const prerequisiteId of quest.prerequisites ) { if ( !hasCompletedQuestV35( prerequisiteId ) ) { return false; } } return true; } //================================================ // GET QUEST STATE //================================================ function getQuestStateV35( questId ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if (!quest) { return QUEST_STATE_V35 .LOCKED; } if ( hasCompletedQuestV35( questId ) && !quest.repeatable ) { return QUEST_STATE_V35 .COMPLETED; } if ( hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { if ( isQuestReadyV35( questId ) ) { return QUEST_STATE_V35 .READY; } return QUEST_STATE_V35 .ACTIVE; } if ( canStartQuestV35( questId ) ) { return QUEST_STATE_V35 .AVAILABLE; } return QUEST_STATE_V35 .LOCKED; } //================================================ // START QUEST //================================================ function startQuestV35( questId, options = {} ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if ( !quest || !canStartQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } if ( quest.repeatable && hasCompletedQuestV35( questId ) ) { QuestSystemV35 .completedQuests = QuestSystemV35 .completedQuests .filter( id => id !== questId ); } resetQuestProgressV35( questId ); QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests .push( questId ); if ( !QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId ) { QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId = questId; } emitQuestEventV35( "quest_started", { quest, options } ); return true; } //================================================ // RESET QUEST PROGRESS //================================================ function resetQuestProgressV35( questId ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if (!quest) { return false; } for ( const objective of quest.objectives ) { objective.progress = 0; objective.completed = false; } return true; } //================================================ // IS OBJECTIVE COMPLETE //================================================ function isQuestObjectiveCompleteV35( objective ) { if (!objective) { return false; } return ( objective.completed || objective.progress >= objective.required ); } //================================================ // IS QUEST READY //================================================ function isQuestReadyV35( questId ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if ( !quest || !hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } if ( quest.objectives.length === 0 ) { return true; } return quest.objectives.every( objective => isQuestObjectiveCompleteV35( objective ) ); } //================================================ // UPDATE OBJECTIVE //================================================ function updateQuestObjectiveV35( questId, objectiveId, amount = 1 ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if ( !quest || !hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } const objective = quest.objectives.find( item => item.id === objectiveId ); if ( !objective || objective.completed ) { return false; } const oldProgress = objective.progress; objective.progress = Math.min( objective.required, objective.progress + amount ); if ( objective.progress >= objective.required ) { objective.completed = true; emitQuestEventV35( "objective_completed", { quest, objective } ); } if ( oldProgress !== objective.progress ) { emitQuestEventV35( "objective_progress", { quest, objective } ); } if ( isQuestReadyV35( questId ) ) { emitQuestEventV35( "quest_ready", { quest } ); if ( quest.autoComplete ) { completeQuestV35( questId ); } } return true; } //================================================ // UPDATE OBJECTIVE BY TYPE //================================================ function updateQuestObjectivesByTypeV35( type, target, amount = 1 ) { for ( const questId of QuestSystemV35.activeQuests ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if (!quest) { continue; } for ( const objective of quest.objectives ) { if ( objective.completed ) { continue; } if ( objective.type !== type ) { continue; } if ( objective.target !== target ) { continue; } updateQuestObjectiveV35( questId, objective.id, amount ); } } } //================================================ // COMPLETE QUEST //================================================ function completeQuestV35( questId ) { const quest = getQuestV35( questId ); if ( !quest || !hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) || !isQuestReadyV35( questId ) ) { return false; } QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests = QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests .filter( id => id !== questId ); if ( !QuestSystemV35 .completedQuests .includes( questId ) ) { QuestSystemV35 .completedQuests .push( questId ); } giveQuestRewardsV35( quest ); if ( QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId === questId ) { QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId = QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests[0] || null; } emitQuestEventV35( "quest_completed", { quest } ); return true; } //================================================ // GIVE QUEST REWARDS //================================================ function giveQuestRewardsV35( quest ) { if (!quest) { return; } const rewards = quest.rewards || {}; if ( Number.isFinite( rewards.gold ) ) { if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.addGold === "function" ) { window.addGold( rewards.gold ); } else { QuestSystemV35 .pendingRewards .push( { type: "gold", amount: rewards.gold, questId: quest.id } ); } } if ( Number.isFinite( rewards.exp ) ) { if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.addExperience === "function" ) { window.addExperience( rewards.exp ); } else { QuestSystemV35 .pendingRewards .push( { type: "exp", amount: rewards.exp, questId: quest.id } ); } } if ( Array.isArray( rewards.items ) ) { for ( const item of rewards.items ) { if ( typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.addItemToInventory === "function" ) { window.addItemToInventory( item.id, item.amount || 1 ); } else { QuestSystemV35 .pendingRewards .push( { type: "item", item, questId: quest.id } ); } } } emitQuestEventV35( "quest_rewards", { quest, rewards } ); } //================================================ // FAIL QUEST //================================================ function failQuestV35( questId ) { if ( !hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests = QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests .filter( id => id !== questId ); if ( !QuestSystemV35 .failedQuests .includes( questId ) ) { QuestSystemV35 .failedQuests .push( questId ); } if ( QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId === questId ) { QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId = QuestSystemV35 .activeQuests[0] || null; } emitQuestEventV35( "quest_failed", { quest: getQuestV35( questId ) } ); return true; } //================================================ // TRACK QUEST //================================================ function trackQuestV35( questId ) { if ( !hasActiveQuestV35( questId ) ) { return false; } QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId = questId; emitQuestEventV35( "quest_tracked", { quest: getQuestV35( questId ) } ); return true; } //================================================ // GET TRACKED QUEST //================================================ function getTrackedQuestV35() { return getQuestV35( QuestSystemV35 .trackedQuestId ); } //================================================ // GET NPC QUESTS //================================================ function getNPCQuestsV35( npcId ) { return getAllQuestsV35() .filter( quest => quest.giverNPC === npcId || quest.turnInNPC === npcId ); } //================================================ // GET NPC AVAILABLE QUESTS //================================================ function getNPCAvailableQuestsV35( npcId ) { return getAllQuestsV35() .filter( quest => quest.giverNPC === npcId && getQuestStateV35( quest.id ) === QUEST_STATE_V35 .AVAILABLE ); } //================================================ // GET NPC READY QUESTS //================================================ function getNPCReadyQuestsV35( npcId ) { return getAllQuestsV35() .filter( quest => quest.turnInNPC === npcId && getQuestStateV35( quest.id ) === QUEST_STATE_V35 .READY ); } //================================================ // GET NPC QUEST MARKER //================================================ function getNPCQuestMarkerV35( npcOrId ) { const npcId = typeof npcOrId === "string" ? npcOrId : ( npcOrId?.definitionId || npcOrId?.id ); * npc.greetings.length ) ]; } if ( npc.dialogue.length > 0 ) { const text = npc.dialogue[ npc.dialogueIndex % npc.dialogue.length ]; npc.dialogueIndex++; return text; } return "..."; } //================================================ function interactWithFriendlyNPCV37( npc, player = null ) { if ( !npc || !npc.canInteract ) { return false; } const now = Date.now(); if ( now - //================================================ function updateFriendlyNPCWanderV37( npc, delta ) { if ( !npc || npc.state === "interacting" ) { return; } npc.wanderTimer -= delta; const distance = Math.hypot( npc.currentTargetX - npc.x, npc.currentTargetY - npc.y ); if ( npc.wanderTimer <= 0 || distance < 3 ) { npc.wanderTimer = 2000 + Math.random() * 5000; const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2; const radius = Math.random() * npc.wanderRadius; npc.currentTargetX = npc.spawnX + Math.cos( angle ) * radius; npc.currentTargetY = npc.spawnY + Math.sin( angle ) * radius; } if ( distance <= 3 ) { npc.state = "idle"; return; } npc.state = "walking"; const dx = npc.currentTargetX - npc.x; const dy = npc.currentTargetY - npc.y; const length = Math.hypot( dx, dy ); if ( length <= 0 ) { return; } const speed = npc.speed * ( delta / 16.6667 ); npc.x += ( dx / length ) * speed; npc.y += ( dy / length ) * speed; if ( Math.abs( dx ) > Math.abs( dy ) ) { npc.direction = dx > 0 ? "right" : "left"; } else { npc.direction = dy > 0 ? //================================================ // FRIENDLY NPC TIME PERIOD V37 //================================================ function getFriendlyNPCTimePeriodV37( hour ) { if ( hour >= 5 && hour < 8 ) { return "morning"; } //================================================ // GET NPC SCHEDULE ENTRY V37 //================================================ function getFriendlyNPCScheduleEntryV37( npc, hour ) { if ( !npc ) { return null; } let schedule = npc.schedule; if ( !schedule || Object.keys( schedule ).length === 0 ) { schedule = createDefaultFriendlyNPCScheduleV37( npc ); npc.schedule = schedule; } let selected = null; let selectedHour = -1; break; case FriendlyNPCScheduleStateV37 .WAKE: npc.state = "idle"; if ( npc.baseSpeed ) { npc.speed = npc.baseSpeed; } npc.mood = FriendlyNPCMoodV37 .NORMAL; break; case FriendlyNPCScheduleStateV37 .WORK: npc.state = "working"; //================================================ // GET PLAYER RELATIONSHIP DATA V37 //================================================ function getFriendlyNPCRelationshipDataV37( npcId ) { if ( !npcId ) { return null; } return FriendlyNPCRelationshipSystemV37 .playerData[ npcId ]; } //================================================ // GET RELATIONSHIP LEVEL FROM POINTS V37 //================================================ function getFriendlyNPCRelationLevelFromPointsV37( points ) { const thresholds = FriendlyNPCRelationshipSystemV37 .relationThresholds; const nextThreshold = FriendlyNPCRelationshipSystemV37 .relationThresholds[ nextLevel ]; if ( currentLevel >= FriendlyNPCRelationLevelV37 .BEST_FRIEND ) { return { current: data.points, needed: data.points, progress: 1, max: true }; } const range = Math.max( 1, nextThreshold - currentThreshold ); return { current: data.points, needed: nextThreshold, progress: Math.max( 0, Math.min( 1, ( data.points - currentThreshold ) / range ) ), max: false };
